{
  "title": "Add aggregated worst-state traffic-light widget (higher_is_worse, inclusive thresholds)",
  "description": "## Goal\nImplement a new PHP dashboard widget that aggregates multiple user-selected **supported numeric items** into **one** traffic-light state using fixed MVP semantics:\n\n- **Aggregation mode (fixed):** `worst-state-wins`\n  - any per-item **red** => widget **red**\n  - else any per-item **yellow** => widget **yellow**\n  - else all per-item **green** => widget **green**\n- **Threshold direction (fixed invariant):** `higher_is_worse` (higher values are worse)\n- **Boundary behavior (inclusive):** threshold comparisons are **inclusive**\n- **Per-item evaluation (inclusive, higher_is_worse):**\n  - `value >= red_threshold` => per-item **red**\n  - else if `value >= yellow_threshold` => per-item **yellow**\n  - else => per-item **green**\n\nOn refresh, the widget must evaluate each selected item’s **latest numeric value** and render the aggregated traffic-light using the framework’s **standard widget covers** for required failure modes.\n\n## Scope\n1. Create **one** new widget type under `ui/widgets/<new_widget_id>/` in the existing PHP UI widget framework.\n2. Register it so it participates in all standard widget flows:\n   - add/edit/save/load\n   - permission-aware render/refresh\n   - persistence/serialization\n3. Implement widget-specific configuration fields using framework-standard field types:\n   - standard widget title/name field\n   - multiselect of **one or more items** (UI filtered/enforced to supported numeric items)\n   - shared `yellow_threshold` (float input)\n   - shared `red_threshold` (float input)\n   - `direction` is **fixed** to `higher_is_worse`:\n     - do not expose to UI (hide/disable)\n     - **server-validate invariant**: reject/cover if client/API attempts to set/override it\n4. Implement server-side validation during render/refresh (and save-time if supported by the framework):\n   - parse thresholds as floats\n   - enforce invariant compatible with fixed classification semantics:\n     - **`red_threshold >= yellow_threshold`** (inclusive ordering)\n   - reject invalid config using the framework’s **invalid configuration** standard cover\n5. Enforce supported-item subset:\n   - Only framework-supported numeric item value types are eligible.\n   - Document (in widget code/help text) the exact subset enforced.\n   - Any selected item outside this subset must be treated as an unsupported selection and handled via standard widget covers (no partial leakage of computed state).\n\n## System Changes\n### Backend\n- Create `ui/widgets/<new_widget_id>/` including widget manifest/registration artifacts as required by the framework (widget id/namespace, routing/actions, config schema bindings).\n- Implement PHP widget controller/view logic for:\n  - load/edit/save config\n  - permission-aware refresh rendering\n  - evaluation/aggregation for latest numeric values\n- Implement server-side aggregation pipeline on refresh:\n  1. Load persisted config\n  2. Validate invariants:\n     - direction fixed to `higher_is_worse`; if config payload contains/overrides direction => **invalid configuration cover**\n     - parse `yellow_threshold` and `red_threshold` as floats\n     - enforce `red_threshold >= yellow_threshold`\n  3. Permission & supported-item filtering:\n     - verify current user can access each selected item\n     - filter to only supported numeric value types\n     - if no items remain after filtering (or the framework treats this as failure), render standard **no-data/unsupported** cover as applicable\n  4. Fetch latest numeric values for remaining item ids using existing framework services (prefer batching if available).\n  5. If any selected item’s latest numeric value is missing/empty/unavailable, use the framework’s standard **no-data** cover.\n  6. Per-item classification (inclusive, higher_is_worse):\n     - `red` if `value >= red_threshold`\n     - else `yellow` if `value >= yellow_threshold`\n     - else `green`\n  7. Aggregate via worst-state-wins:\n     - any `red` => widget `red`\n     - else any `yellow` => widget `yellow`\n     - else `green`\n  8. Render final traffic-light state using framework-standard traffic-light widget/state rendering and required standard covers for failure modes.\n- Ensure persistence/serialization:\n  - widget config fields round-trip correctly through the framework widget persistence mechanism.\n\n### Frontend\n- Add widget configuration UI using framework-standard field types:\n  - title/name\n  - multiselect for item ids (restricted to supported numeric items)\n  - numeric inputs for `yellow_threshold` and `red_threshold` (float)\n  - direction: hidden/disabled (cannot be set/overridden by users)\n- If the framework uses JS-based refresh:\n  - reuse/align with existing widget refresh patterns (request refresh/update via standard widget update mechanism).\n- Ensure client-side behavior mirrors server invariants where feasible:\n  - if invalid thresholds ordering occurs, rely on server invalid-config cover on refresh (do not implement divergent semantics).\n\n### Failure modes (must use standard covers)\n- Invalid configuration (e.g., missing thresholds, parse failure, or `red_threshold < yellow_threshold`, or direction override attempt)\n- No items selected\n- All selected items unsupported or inaccessible to the current user\n- Missing/empty latest numeric value(s) required for evaluation\n- Permission issues (ensure no leakage of item state)\n\n## Acceptance Criteria\n- A new widget type is available in the dashboard widget picker.\n- Widget config UI allows selecting **1+ supported numeric items** and setting `yellow_threshold` and `red_threshold` as floats.\n- On refresh, the widget:\n  - fetches each selected item’s latest numeric value\n  - classifies each item inclusively using **higher_is_worse** semantics\n  - aggregates using **worst-state-wins** to render exactly one traffic-light state.\n- If `red_threshold < yellow_threshold` (or thresholds can’t be parsed as floats), the widget shows the framework’s **invalid configuration** cover.\n- If `direction` is provided/modified via API/client payload, the widget shows the **invalid configuration** cover (server enforces fixed invariant).\n- Unsupported/non-numeric items or inaccessible items do not produce partial/incorrect traffic-light results; the widget uses the framework’s **standard covers**.\n- Widget configuration persists across save/reload and participates in standard add/edit/save/load and permission-aware refresh flows.\n\n## Implementation Checklist\n### Backend\n- [ ] Create widget directory: `ui/widgets/<new_widget_id>/` with required framework manifest/routing/registration.\n- [ ] {
  "title": "Add worst-state traffic-light widget (higher_is_worse, inclusive thresholds)",
  "description": "## Goal\nImplement a new PHP dashboard widget that aggregates multiple user-selected **supported numeric items** into **one** traffic-light state using fixed MVP semantics:\n\n- **Aggregation mode (fixed):** `worst-state-wins`\n  - any per-item **red** => widget **red**\n  - else any per-item **yellow** => widget **yellow**\n  - else all per-item **green** => widget **green**\n- **Threshold direction (fixed invariant):** `higher_is_worse` (higher values are worse)\n- **Boundary behavior (inclusive):** threshold comparisons are **inclusive**\n- **Per-item evaluation (inclusive, higher_is_worse):**\n  - `value >= red_threshold` => per-item **red**\n  - else if `value >= yellow_threshold` => per-item **yellow**\n  - else => per-item **green**\n\nOn refresh, the widget must evaluate each selected item’s **latest numeric value** and render the aggregated traffic-light using the framework’s **standard widget covers** for required failure modes.\n\n## Scope\n1. Create **one** new widget type under `ui/widgets/<new_widget_id>/` in the existing PHP UI widget framework.\n2. Register it so it participates in all standard widget flows:\n   - add/edit/save/load\n   - permission-aware render/refresh\n   - persistence/serialization\n3. Implement widget-specific configuration fields using framework-standard field types:\n   - standard widget title/name field\n   - multiselect of **one or more items** (UI filtered/enforced to supported numeric items)\n   - shared `yellow_threshold` (float input)\n   - shared `red_threshold` (float input)\n   - `direction` is **fixed** to `higher_is_worse`:\n     - do not expose to UI (hide/disable)\n     - **server-validate invariant**: reject/cover if client/API attempts to set/override it\n4. Implement server-side validation during render/refresh (and save-time if supported by the framework):\n   - parse thresholds as floats\n   - enforce invariant compatible with fixed classification semantics:\n     - **`red_threshold >= yellow_threshold`** (inclusive ordering)\n   - reject invalid config using the framework’s **invalid configuration** standard cover\n5. Enforce supported-item subset:\n   - Only framework-supported numeric item value types are eligible.\n   - Document (in widget code/help text) the exact subset enforced.\n   - Any selected item outside this subset must be treated as an unsupported selection and handled via standard widget covers (no partial aggregation).\n6. Refresh-time behavior:\n   - Fetch each selected item’s latest numeric value via the framework’s data access layer, respecting permission/auth context.\n   - Compute per-item classification with the inclusive/higher_is_worse rules.\n   - Aggregate via worst-state-wins.\n   - Render a single traffic-light UI state (green/yellow/red) or return the correct standard cover on failure modes (invalid config, unsupported selection, missing/unavailable value, permissions).\n\n## System Changes\nBackend changes:\n- Add new widget implementation at `ui/widgets/<new_widget_id>/` following existing PHP widget framework conventions (manifest/registration, controller/handler, view renderer if applicable).\n- Register widget metadata/manifest so it participates in standard widget flows and refresh endpoints.\n- Implement config parsing/validation:\n  - thresholds: float parsing\n  - invariant: `red_threshold >= yellow_threshold`\n  - direction immutability: reject/cover if any payload/config attempts to override direction away from `higher_is_worse`\n- Implement supported-item enforcement:\n  - use the framework’s established supported numeric item subset mechanism (value type constraints)\n  - if any selected item is unsupported, return standard unsupported/invalid selection cover\n- Implement refresh evaluation pipeline:\n  - load persisted config\n  - validate config\n  - validate all selected items are supported\n  - fetch latest numeric values (permission-aware)\n  - handle missing/unavailable values using framework-standard covers\n  - compute per-item color with inclusive thresholds\n  - aggregate with worst-state-wins\n  - render aggregated traffic-light UI\n\nFrontend/UI changes:\n- Add widget edit/config UI using framework-standard field components:\n  - title/name field\n  - item multiselect limited to supported numeric items\n  - `yellow_threshold` float input\n  - `red_threshold` float input\n- Ensure `direction` is not exposed and cannot be set by client/API:\n  - omit from the payload, or enforce fixed value server-side and reject invalid attempts per framework patterns\n- (Optional but recommended) add client-side validation mirroring server invariant `red_threshold >= yellow_threshold`.\n\nTesting changes:\n- Unit tests for:\n  - inclusive classification boundaries (exact equality at thresholds)\n  - worst-state-wins aggregation\n  - config validation invariants (`red_threshold >= yellow_threshold`, direction immutability)\n  - supported-item subset enforcement (unsupported item => unsupported cover)\n- Integration/API tests for:\n  - widget config persistence round-trip\n  - refresh endpoint returns correct traffic-light state\n  - refresh endpoint returns correct standard covers for failure modes\n  - permission-aware behavior (no leakage; correct covers)\n- UI/workbook/E2E coverage where the repo supports it.\n\n## Acceptance Criteria\n- A new widget type exists and can be added via the standard widget workflow (add/edit/save/load).\n- Given selected supported numeric items and latest numeric values, the widget computes:\n  - per-item color using **inclusive** thresholds and **higher_is_worse** semantics\n  - aggregated widget color using **worst-state-wins** exactly as specified\n- Direction is fixed to `higher_is_worse`:\n  - any attempt to override direction via client/API results in invalid configuration handling via the framework’s standard cover (or is ignored in a way that still guarantees the fixed invariant)\n- Server-side validation enforces `red_threshold >= yellow_threshold`:\n  - invalid config returns the framework’s **invalid configuration** standard cover\n- Only framework-supported numeric item types are accepted:\n  - selecting unsupported items results in the standard unsupported/invalid selection cover (no partial aggregation)\n- On refresh, the widget fetches latest values permission-aware and renders:\n  - aggregated traffic-light success state, OR\n  - appropriate standard cover for missing/unavailable values/unapproved access/invalid config\n\n## Implementation Checklist\n### Backend\n- [ ] Create `ui/widgets/<new_widget_id>/` with the widget implementation following existing framework structure.\n- [ ] Add/extend widget manifest/registration so it participates in standard flows (add/edit/save/load, persistence, refresh).\n- [ ] Implement config schema/field parsing for:\n  - selected item multiselect (store list of item identifiers)\n  - `yellow_threshold` (float)\n  - `red_threshold` (float)\n  - title/name\n- [ ] Implement server-side validation on save and/or refresh (per framework capability):\n  - parse floats\n  - enforce `red_threshold >= yellow_threshold` (inclusive)\n  - enforce fixed invariant for direction (`higher_is_worse` only) and reject/cover on override attempts\n- [ ] Enforce supported numeric item subset:\n  - document exact subset in widget help/text/code\n  - ensure unsupported selections trigger standard unsupported/invalid selection cover\n- [ ] Implement refresh-time latest-value evaluation:\n  - permission-aware fetch of latest numeric value per selected item\n  - handle missing/unavailable values via standard covers\n  - compute per-item color using:\n    - red if `value >= red_threshold`\n    - else yellow if `value >= yellow_threshold`\n    - else green\n  - aggregate via worst-state-wins:\n    - any red => widget red, else any yellow => widget yellow, else green\n- [ ] Implement/attach rendering for the single aggregated traffic-light state.\n\n### Frontend\n- [ ] Add widget configuration UI:\n  - title/name field (standard)\n  - item multiselect restricted to supported numeric items\n  - `yellow_threshold` float input\n  - `red_threshold` float input\n- [ ] Do not expose `direction` in UI.\n- [ ] Add client-side validation mirroring server invariant (`red_threshold >= yellow_threshold`) if framework patterns allow.\n- [ ] Ensure refresh displays aggregated state and shows standard covers returned by backend.\n\n### Testing\n- [ ] Unit tests:\n  - per-item inclusive boundary classification\n  - worst-state-wins aggregation\n  - validation rules (`red_threshold >= yellow_threshold`, direction immutability)\n  - unsupported-item selection handling\n- [ ] Integration/API tests:\n  - save/load config persistence for the new widget\n  - refresh endpoint returns correct aggregated state based on latest mocked values\n  - refresh returns standard covers for:\n    - invalid thresholds\n    - unsupported item selections\n    - missing/unavailable values\n    - permission-denied contexts\n- [ ] UI tests (if repo has them): widget config flow + state update behavior.\n- [ ] Manual verification:\n  - create widget, select multiple items, and confirm traffic-light changes correctly when item values cross thresholds (including exact equality at thresholds)\n  - Manual verification: verify worst-state-wins behavior (one red among many => widget red)\n  - Manual verification: attempt to submit invalid config (`red_threshold < yellow_threshold`) and confirm the correct invalid configuration cover appears\n  - Manual verification: attempt to include unsupported item types and confirm the correct unsupported/invalid selection cover appears\n  - Manual verification: craft a request to try overriding direction and confirm invalid configuration handling/cover appears as required\n\n## Verification\n- Automated/unit verification (runs in CI):\n  - inclusive classification correctness at `value == yellow_threshold` and `value == red_threshold`\n  - aggregation correctness with mixed per-item states\n  - config invariant rejection (`red_threshold >= yellow_threshold`) and direction immutability\n- Automated/integration verification (runs in CI):\n  - refresh endpoint behavior returns exact standard covers for invalid/unsupported/missing/permission cases\n- Manual verification:\n  - Visual confirmation that only one aggregated traffic-light is rendered and matches the computed state\n\n## Documentation Impact\n- Add/extend widget help text/code comments documenting:\n  - the exact supported numeric item value subset enforced\n  - fixed semantics: `higher_is_worse`, inclusive thresholds, worst-state-wins aggregation\n  - validation/invariant rules: `red_threshold >= yellow_threshold` and direction immutability\n- If the repo has a widget catalog/list, add an entry/translation key for the new widget (per existing mechanisms).\n\n## Suggested Diagram\n```puml\nleft to right direction\nskinparam shadowing false\n\nactor User as U\n\nrectangle \"Widget refresh handler\\n(widget.<new_widget_id>.refresh)\" as Refresh {\n  [*] --> \"Load persisted widget config\"\n  --> \"Server-validate config\\n- parse float thresholds\\n- enforce invariant: red_threshold >= yellow_threshold (inclusive)\\n- enforce direction fixed higher_is_worse\\n- unsupported/empty selection cover per framework\"\n  --> \"Enforce supported-item subset\\n(selected item value types allowed?)\"\n  --> \"If unsupported: return standard unsupported/invalid selection cover\"\n  --> \"Fetch latest numeric value per selected item\\n(permission-aware via framework/data layer)\"\n  --> \"If missing/unavailable values: return standard failure cover\"\n  --> \"Per-item classify (higher_is_worse, inclusive):\\nif value >= red_threshold => RED\\nelse if value >= yellow_threshold => YELLOW\\nelse GREEN\"\n  --> \"Aggregate worst-state-wins:\\nany RED => RED\\nelse any YELLOW => YELLOW\\nelse GREEN\"\n  --> \"Render success traffic-light UI\\n(single aggregated state)\"\n}\n\nrectangle \"Standard cover paths\" as Covers {\n  [*] --> \"Invalid config cover\"\n  [*] --> \"Unsupported selection cover\"\n  [*] --> \"Missing/unavailable value cover\"\n}\n\nU --> Refresh\nRefresh --> Covers : on error/invalid/unsupported\nRefresh --> \"Success render\" : aggregated state\n```",
  "codex_prompt": "## Goal\nImplement a new PHP dashboard widget that aggregates multiple user-selected **supported numeric items** into **one** traffic-light state using fixed MVP semantics:\n\n- **Aggregation mode (fixed):** `worst-state-wins`\n  - any per-item **red** => widget **red**\n  - else any per-item **yellow** => widget **yellow**\n  - else all per-item **green** => widget **green**\n- **Threshold direction (fixed invariant):** `higher_is_worse` (higher values are worse)\n- **Boundary behavior (inclusive):** threshold comparisons are **inclusive**\n- **Per-item evaluation (inclusive, higher_is_worse):**\n  - `value >= red_threshold` => per-item **red**\n  - else if `value >= yellow_threshold` => per-item **yellow**\n  - else => per-item **green**\n\nOn refresh, the widget must evaluate each selected item’s **latest numeric value** and render the aggregated traffic-light using the framework’s **standard widget covers** for required failure modes.\n\n## Scope\n1. Create **one** new widget type under `ui/widgets/<new_widget_id>/` in the existing PHP UI widget framework.\n2. Register it so it participates in all standard widget flows:\n   - add/edit/save/load\n   - permission-aware render/refresh\n   - persistence/serialization\n3. Implement widget-specific configuration fields using framework-standard field types:\n   - standard widget title/name field\n   - multiselect of **one or more items** (UI filtered/enforced to supported numeric items)\n   - shared `yellow_threshold` (float input)\n   - shared `red_threshold` (float input)\n   - `direction` is **fixed** to `higher_is_worse`:\n     - do not expose to UI (hide/disable)\n     - **server-validate invariant**: reject/cover if client/API attempts to set/override it\n4. Implement server-side validation during render/refresh (and save-time if supported by the framework):\n   - parse thresholds as floats\n   - enforce invariant compatible with fixed classification semantics:\n     - **`red_threshold >= yellow_threshold`** (inclusive ordering)\n   - reject invalid config using the framework’s **invalid configuration** standard cover\n5. Enforce supported-item subset:\n   - Only framework-supported numeric item value types are eligible.\n   - Document (in widget code/help text) the exact subset enforced.\n   - Any selected item outside this subset must be treated as an unsupported selection and handled via standard widget covers (no partial aggregation).\n6. Refresh-time behavior:\n   - Fetch each selected item’s latest numeric value via the framework’s data access layer, respecting permission/auth context.\n   - Compute per-item classification with the inclusive/higher_is_worse rules.\n   - Aggregate via worst-state-wins.\n   - Render a single traffic-light UI state (green/yellow/red) or return the correct standard cover on failure modes (invalid config, unsupported selection, missing/unavailable value, permissions).\n\n## System Changes\nBackend changes:\n- Add new widget implementation at `ui/widgets/<new_widget_id>/` following existing PHP widget framework conventions (manifest/registration, controller/handler, view renderer if applicable).\n- Register widget metadata/manifest so it participates in standard widget flows and refresh endpoints.\n- Implement config parsing/validation:\n  - thresholds: float parsing\n  - invariant: `red_threshold >= yellow_threshold`\n  - direction immutability: reject/cover if any payload/config attempts to override direction away from `higher_is_worse`\n- Implement supported-item enforcement:\n  - use the framework’s established supported numeric item subset mechanism (value type constraints)\n  - if any selected item is unsupported, return standard unsupported/invalid selection cover\n- Implement refresh evaluation pipeline:\n  - load persisted config\n  - validate config\n  - validate all selected items are supported\n  - fetch latest numeric values (permission-aware)\n  - handle missing/unavailable values using framework-standard covers\n  - compute per-item color with inclusive thresholds\n  - aggregate with worst-state-wins\n  - render aggregated traffic-light UI\n\nFrontend/UI changes:\n- Add widget edit/config UI using framework-standard field components:\n  - title/name field\n  - item multiselect limited to supported numeric items\n  - `yellow_threshold` float input\n  - `red_threshold` float input\n- Ensure `direction` is not exposed and cannot be set by client/API:\n  - omit from the payload, or enforce fixed value server-side and reject invalid attempts per framework patterns\n- (Optional but recommended) add client-side validation mirroring server invariant `red_threshold >= yellow_threshold`.\n\nTesting changes:\n- Unit tests for:\n  - inclusive classification boundaries (exact equality at thresholds)\n  - worst-state-wins aggregation\n  - config validation invariants (`red_threshold >= yellow_threshold`, direction immutability)\n  - supported-item subset enforcement (unsupported item => unsupported cover)\n- Integration/API tests for:\n  - widget config persistence round-trip\n  - refresh endpoint returns correct traffic-light state\n  - refresh endpoint returns correct standard covers for failure modes\n  - permission-aware behavior (no leakage; correct covers)\n- UI/workbook/E2E coverage where the repo supports it.\n\n## Acceptance Criteria\n- A new widget type exists and can be added via the standard widget workflow (add/edit/save/load).\n- Given selected supported numeric items and latest numeric values, the widget computes:\n  - per-item color using **inclusive** thresholds and **higher_is_worse** semantics\n  - aggregated widget color using **worst-state-wins** exactly as specified\n- Direction is fixed to `higher_is_worse`:\n  - any attempt to override direction via client/API results in invalid configuration handling via the framework’s standard cover (or is ignored in a way that still guarantees the fixed invariant)\n- Server-side validation enforces `red_threshold >= yellow_threshold`:\n  - invalid config returns the framework’s **invalid configuration** standard cover\n- Only framework-supported numeric item types are accepted:\n  - selecting unsupported items results in the standard unsupported/invalid selection cover (no partial aggregation)\n- On refresh, the widget fetches latest values permission-aware and renders:\n  - aggregated traffic-light success state, OR\n  - appropriate standard cover for missing/unavailable values/unapproved access/invalid config\n\n## Implementation Checklist\n### Backend\n- [ ] Create `ui/widgets/<new_widget_id>/` with the widget implementation following existing framework structure.\n- [ ] Add/extend widget manifest/registration so it participates in standard flows (add/edit/save/load, persistence, refresh).\n- [ ] Implement config schema/field parsing for:\n  - selected item multiselect (store list of item identifiers)\n  - `yellow_threshold` (float)\n  - `red_threshold` (float)\n  - title/name\n- [ ] Implement server-side validation on save and/or refresh (per framework capability):\n  - parse floats\n  - enforce `red_threshold >= yellow_threshold` (inclusive)\n  - enforce fixed invariant for direction (`higher_is_worse` only) and reject/cover on override attempts\n- [ ] Enforce supported numeric item subset:\n  - document exact subset in widget help/text/code\n  - ensure unsupported selections trigger standard unsupported/invalid selection cover\n- [ ] Implement refresh-time latest-value evaluation:\n  - permission-aware fetch of latest numeric value per selected item\n  - handle missing/unavailable values via standard covers\n  - compute per-item color using:\n    - red if `value >= red_threshold`\n    - else yellow if `value >= yellow_threshold`\n    - else green\n  - aggregate via worst-state-wins:\n    - any red => widget red, else any yellow => widget yellow, else green\n- [ ] Implement/attach rendering for the single aggregated traffic-light state.\n\n### Frontend\n- [ ] Add widget configuration UI:\n  - title/name field (standard)\n  - item multiselect restricted to supported numeric items\n  - `yellow_threshold` float input\n  - `red_threshold` float input\n- [ ] Do not expose `direction` in UI.\n- [ ] Add client-side validation mirroring server invariant (`red_threshold >= yellow_threshold`) if framework patterns allow.\n- [ ] Ensure refresh displays aggregated state and shows standard covers returned by backend.\n\n### Testing\n- [ ] Unit tests:\n  - per-item inclusive boundary classification\n  - worst-state-wins aggregation\n  - validation rules (`red_threshold >= yellow_threshold`, direction immutability)\n  - unsupported-item selection handling\n- [ ] Integration/API tests:\n  - save/load config persistence for the new widget\n  - refresh endpoint returns correct aggregated state based on latest mocked values\n  - refresh returns standard covers for:\n    - invalid thresholds\n    - unsupported item selections\n    - missing/unavailable values\n    - permission-denied contexts\n- [ ] UI tests (if repo has them): widget config flow + state update behavior.\n- [ ] Manual verification:\n  - create widget, select multiple items, and confirm traffic-light changes correctly when item values cross thresholds (including exact equality at thresholds)\n  - Manual verification: verify worst-state-wins behavior (one red among many => widget red)\n  - Manual verification: attempt to submit invalid config (`red_threshold < yellow_threshold`) and confirm the correct invalid configuration cover appears\n  - Manual verification: attempt to include unsupported item types and confirm the correct unsupported/invalid selection cover appears\n  - Manual verification: craft a request to try overriding direction and confirm invalid configuration handling/cover appears as required\n\n## Verification\n- Automated/unit verification (runs in CI):\n  - inclusive classification correctness at `value == yellow_threshold` and `value == red_threshold`\n  - aggregation correctness with mixed per-item states\n  - config invariant rejection (`red_threshold >= yellow_threshold`) and direction immutability\n- Automated/integration verification (runs in CI):\n  - refresh endpoint behavior returns exact standard covers for invalid/unsupported/missing/permission cases\n- Manual verification:\n  - Visual confirmation that only one aggregated traffic-light is rendered and matches the computed state\n\n## Documentation Impact\n- Add/extend widget help text/code comments documenting:\n  - the exact supported numeric item value subset enforced\n  - fixed semantics: `higher_is_worse`, inclusive thresholds, worst-state-wins aggregation\n  - validation/invariant rules: `red_threshold >= yellow_threshold` and direction immutability\n- If the repo has a widget catalog/list, add an entry/translation key for the new widget (per existing mechanisms).\n\n## Suggested Diagram\n```puml\nleft to right direction\nskinparam shadowing false\n\nactor User as U\n\nrectangle \"Widget refresh handler\\n(widget.<new_widget_id>.refresh)\" as Refresh {\n  [*] --> \"Load persisted widget config\"\n  --> \"Server-validate config\\n- parse float thresholds\\n- enforce invariant: red_threshold >= yellow_threshold (inclusive)\\n- enforce direction fixed higher_is_worse\\n- unsupported/empty selection cover per framework\"\n  --> \"Enforce supported-item subset\\n(selected item value types allowed?)\"\n  --> \"If unsupported: return standard unsupported/invalid selection cover\"\n  --> \"Fetch latest numeric value per selected item\\n(permission-aware via framework/data layer)\"\n  --> \"If missing/unavailable values: return standard failure cover\"\n  --> \"Per-item classify (higher_is_worse, inclusive):\\nif value >= red_threshold => RED\\nelse if value >= yellow_threshold => YELLOW\\nelse GREEN\"\n  --> \"Aggregate worst-state-wins:\\nany RED => RED\\nelse any YELLOW => YELLOW\\nelse GREEN\"\n  --> \"Render success traffic-light UI\\n(single aggregated state)\"\n}\n\nrectangle \"Standard cover paths\" as Covers {\n  [*] --> \"Invalid config cover\"\n  [*] --> \"Unsupported selection cover\"\n  [*] --> \"Missing/unavailable value cover\"\n}\n\nU --> Refresh\nRefresh --> Covers : on error/invalid/unsupported\nRefresh --> \"Success render\" : aggregated state\n```",
  "rationale": "The refined ticket locks all MVP semantics as fixed invariants (aggregation mode, direction, inclusive thresholds) and makes failure-mode handling explicit by requiring use of framework-standard widget covers. It also clarifies where server-side enforcement must occur (direction immutability, threshold ordering, supported-item subset) and scopes implementation surfaces to one new widget type plus registration, config UI, refresh evaluation, and a focused set of unit/integration/manual checks."
}

handle missing/unavailable values via standard covers\n  - compute per-item color using:\n    - red if `value >= red_threshold`\n    - else yellow if `value >= yellow_threshold`\n    - else green\n  - aggregate via worst-state-wins:\n    - any red => widget red, else any yellow => widget yellow, else green\n- [ ] Implement/attach rendering for the single aggregated traffic-light state.\n\n### Frontend\n- [ ] Add widget configuration UI:\n  - title/name field (standard)\n  - item multiselect restricted to supported numeric items\n  - `yellow_threshold` float input\n  - `red_threshold` float input\n- [ ] Do not expose `direction` in UI.\n- [ ] Add client-side validation mirroring server invariant (`red_threshold >= yellow_threshold`) if framework patterns allow.\n- [ ] Ensure refresh displays aggregated state and shows standard covers returned by backend.\n\n### Testing\n- [ ] Unit tests:\n  - per-item inclusive boundary classification\n  - worst-state-wins aggregation\n  - validation rules (`red_threshold >= yellow_threshold`, direction immutability)\n  - unsupported-item selection handling\n- [ ] Integration/API tests:\n  - save/load config persistence for the new widget\n  - refresh endpoint returns correct aggregated state based on latest mocked values\n  - refresh returns standard covers for:\n    - invalid thresholds\n    - unsupported item selections\n    - missing/unavailable values\n    - permission-denied contexts\n- [ ] UI tests (if repo has them): widget config flow + state update behavior.\n- [ ] Manual verification:\n  - create widget, select multiple items, and confirm traffic-light changes correctly when item values cross thresholds (including exact equality at thresholds)\n  - Manual verification: verify worst-state-wins behavior (one red among many => widget red)\n  - Manual verification: attempt to submit invalid config (`red_threshold < yellow_threshold`) and confirm the correct invalid configuration cover appears\n  - Manual verification: attempt to include unsupported item types and confirm the correct unsupported/invalid selection cover appears\n  - Manual verification: craft a request to try overriding direction and confirm invalid configuration handling/cover appears as required\n\n## Verification\n- Automated/unit verification (runs in CI):\n  - inclusive classification correctness at `value == yellow_threshold` and `value == red_threshold`\n  - aggregation correctness with mixed per-item states\n  - config invariant rejection (`red_threshold >= yellow_threshold`) and direction immutability\n- Automated/integration verification (runs in CI):\n  - refresh endpoint behavior returns exact standard covers for invalid/unsupported/missing/permission cases\n- Manual verification:\n  - Visual confirmation that only one aggregated traffic-light is rendered and matches the computed state\n\n## Documentation Impact\n- Add/extend widget help text/code comments documenting:\n  - the exact supported numeric item value subset enforced\n  - fixed semantics: `higher_is_worse`, inclusive thresholds, worst-state-wins aggregation\n  - validation/invariant rules: `red_threshold >= yellow_threshold` and direction immutability\n- If the repo has a widget catalog/list, add an entry/translation key for the new widget (per existing mechanisms).\n\n## Suggested Diagram\n```puml\nleft to right direction\nskinparam shadowing false\n\nactor User as U\n\nrectangle \"Widget refresh handler\\n(widget.<new_widget_id>.refresh)\" as Refresh {\n  [*] --> \"Load persisted widget config\"\n  --> \"Server-validate config\\n- parse float thresholds\\n- enforce invariant: red_threshold >= yellow_threshold (inclusive)\\n- enforce direction fixed higher_is_worse\\n- unsupported/empty selection cover per framework\"\n  --> \"Enforce supported-item subset\\n(selected item value types allowed?)\"\n  --> \"If unsupported: return standard unsupported/invalid selection cover\"\n  --> \"Fetch latest numeric value per selected item\\n(permission-aware via framework/data layer)\"\n  --> \"If missing/unavailable values: return standard failure cover\"\n  --> \"Per-item classify (higher_is_worse, inclusive):\\nif value >= red_threshold => RED\\nelse if value >= yellow_threshold => YELLOW\\nelse GREEN\"\n  --> \"Aggregate worst-state-wins:\\nany RED => RED\\nelse any YELLOW => YELLOW\\nelse GREEN\"\n  --> \"Render success traffic-light UI\\n(single aggregated state)\"\n}\n\nrectangle \"Standard cover paths\" as Covers {\n  [*] --> \"Invalid config cover\"\n  [*] --> \"Unsupported selection cover\"\n  [*] --> \"Missing/unavailable value cover\"\n}\n\nU --> Refresh\nRefresh --> Covers : on error/invalid/unsupported\nRefresh --> \"Success render\" : aggregated state\n```",
    "rationale": "The refined ticket locks all MVP semantics as fixed invariants (aggregation mode, direction, inclusive thresholds) and makes failure-mode handling explicit by requiring use of framework-standard widget covers. It also clarifies where server-side enforcement must occur (direction immutability, threshold ordering, supported-item subset) and scopes implementation surfaces to one new widget type plus registration, config UI, refresh evaluation, and a focused set of unit/integration/manual checks."
